| **Design stance** This is a logical design intended for implementation planning. Field-level clinical form submissions should store the complete submitted form payload as versioned JSON for auditability _,_ traceability, and reconstruction of the exact submitted form version. JSON payloads are not intended to be used for operational queries, reporting queries, dashboard filters, risk detection, referral logic, or search. All fields required for search, workflow execution, validation, risk detection, referral logic, dashboard filters, and reporting must be extracted into indexed/derived columns, normalized child tables, or reporting views such as form\_answers, risk\_assessments, risk\_flags, referrals, and ClickHouse linelist views. ClickHouse linelist tables expose canonical report field names for ARMMAN analysts. |                                                                                                                                                       |

# **1\. Scope and Assumptions**

- All primary keys are UUIDs. In Postgres, store as BINARY(16) for production efficiency or CHAR(36) during early development if readability is preferred.
- Every mutable operational table should carry created\_at, created\_by, updated\_at, updated\_by, is\_deleted, and deleted\_at unless explicitly immutable.
- PII fields such as names, phones, addresses, RCH number, bank details, PAN, Aadhaar, and account number should be encrypted or tokenized at application level in addition to DB encryption at rest.
- Form submissions retain the raw submitted JSON and reference the exact form\_version\_id and rule\_version\_id used at the time of submission.
- Rules for scheduling, risk, escalation, incentive, and closure are versioned configuration records. Business logic must not be hardcoded in application tables.
- ClickHouse table names and field aliases must match the Manager Dashboard PRD/M\&E linelist where ARMMAN analysts write SQL directly.

# **3\. Relationship Summary**

| Relationship                          | Cardinality              | Implementation note                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :------------------------------------ | :----------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Funder \-\> Project                   | 1 to many                | A project belongs to one funder/donor and one financial-year implementation context.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Project \-\> Geography                | many to many             | Use project\_geographies to scope districts, blocks, villages, and padas.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Supervisor \-\> Sakhi                 | 1 to many over time      | sakhi\_profiles has current supervisor\_id; sakhi\_assignments preserves history and geography.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Beneficiary PII \-\> Beneficiary Case | 1 to many                | Supports re-enrollment/new pregnancy with a new case while preserving duplicate detection and PII reuse.A person-level PII record can be linked to multiple beneficiary cases. For a second pregnancy, including under the same project, the previous pregnancy case is not updated. A new beneficiary\_cases record is created with a new beneficiary\_id/case number and the same pii\_id. All pregnancy-specific data such as registration details, LMP, EDD, delivery, visits, risks, referrals, closure, and reports remain linked to the respective beneficiary case. |
| Mother Case \-\> Child Case           | 1 to many                | Child cases can link to mother\_beneficiary\_id when created through delivery; direct child registrations can be unlinked.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Beneficiary Case \-\> Visit Schedule  | 1 to many                | Schedules are generated offline and synced. ANC/PP/NN/INC are mostly fixed; CCV is generated at transition based on risk state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Visit Instance \-\> Form Submission   | 1 to many                | Delivery event can create delivery, INC/child registration, NN1, and PP1 form submissions in one session, depending on delivery outcome and child eligibility.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Form Submission \-\> Risk Assessment  | 1 to 0/1                 | Risk evaluation runs after submission and stores exact rule\_version\_id.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Risk Assessment \-\> Risk Flags       | 1 to many                | Each flagged condition and grade is stored separately for reporting and referral logic. The parent risk\_assessments record also stores the visit-level overall high-risk indicator/category, so the system can identify whether the visit as a whole is high risk even when multiple individual risk\_flags are present.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Visit/Risk \-\> Referral              | 1 to many                | For a given visit, at most one referral form is generated. The referral may be standard or accompanied. Multiple risk conditions from the same visit may contribute to the same referral record through trigger\_condition\_list\_json or equivalent linked risk-flag references. Follow-ups and approvals are stored as separate records.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Beneficiary \-\> Closure/Reopen       | 1 to many over lifecycle | Closure is auditable; reopen is approval-controlled and limited to permitted reasons.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Operational data \-\> ClickHouse      | batch/near-real-time ETL | Airflow loads facts/dimensions from Postgres to ClickHouse using watermarks and ETL run logs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

# **4\. OLTP Table Definitions**

**4.0 Form, Response, Rule, and Workflow Data Storage Approach**

The design separates configurable form definitions, submitted responses, rule definitions, and workflow records.

1. Questions, options, numeric ranges, mandatory fields, and skip logic are stored in form\_versions.schema\_json and form\_versions.validation\_json, linked to form\_definitions. Each published version of a form stores the exact question set, field codes, labels, input types, dropdown source, numeric min/max range, required flags, validation rules, and conditional/skip logic.
2. Actual submitted answers are stored in form\_submissions.form\_data\_json as the complete submitted payload for audit/replay. Searchable, reportable, or rule-critical answers are additionally stored in form\_answers using the stable field\_code / question identifier.
3. Risk and referral rules are stored in rule\_sets and rule\_versions. Risk condition metadata is stored in risk\_conditions. Risk evaluation output is stored in risk\_assessments and risk\_flags.
4. Referral and referral follow-up workflow records are stored in referrals and referral\_followups. Their form questions/answers are still stored through form\_definitions, form\_versions, form\_submissions, and form\_answers. The workflow tables store structured status, date, facility, approval, follow-up, and outcome fields.

This approach keeps forms and rules version-controlled while preserving structured workflow records for operations and reporting.

5. Form metadata, validation metadata, and business rules are version-controlled and auditable. Each published form version stores question definitions, data types, mandatory rules, option sources, numeric ranges, and skip logic in schema\_json and validation\_json. Each published rule version stores schedule, risk, referral, escalation, closure, and incentive logic in rule\_versions.rules\_json. Changes to form versions and rule versions must be tracked through published\_by\_user\_id, effective\_from, effective\_to, checksum, and audit\_logs so the system can reconstruct which form and rule version was active for any submitted record.
6. Database-level validations will be used for stable structural constraints such as NOT NULL fields, FK integrity, uniqueness, enum/check constraints, date ordering, and valid status transitions. Dynamic clinical validations, skip logic, high-risk rules, referral rules, and schedule rules will remain in the versioned rule engine because they are configurable and may change by program version. To detect failures early, the platform will run data-quality and reconciliation checks after sync/form submission to identify missing mandatory answers, invalid values, failed rule evaluation, missing risk\_assessment records, missing referral records where referral-triggering risk exists, duplicate submissions, and sync anomalies. These exceptions should be logged and alerted through operational monitoring.

| Requirement                                                      | Table(s) Used                                                | What Is Stored                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| :--------------------------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Form/question master                                             | form\_definitions                                            | Logical form name/code, e.g. ANC, referral, referral follow-up, closure                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Question details, options, ranges, mandatory rules, skip pattern | form\_versions.schema\_json, form\_versions.validation\_json | Versioned form schema, question codes, labels, input types, dropdown options, min/max ranges, required flags, conditional display/skip logic                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Dropdown options reused across forms                             | lookup\_categories, lookup\_values                           | Master options such as gender, visit status, referral type, closure reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Submitted form response                                          | form\_submissions                                            | One submitted form instance, raw JSON payload, form version, beneficiary, visit, submitter                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Individual question response                                     | form\_answers                                                | Normalized answer values for searchable/reportable/rule-critical fields                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Risk rule master                                                 | rule\_sets, rule\_versions                                   | Versioned risk/schedule/referral/escalation/closure/incentive rules                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Risk condition master                                            | risk\_conditions                                             | Risk condition code, name, phase, grade scale, default referral/education flags                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Risk evaluation result                                           | risk\_assessments, risk\_flags                               | Overall visit risk in risk\_assessments and one row per evaluated risk condition in risk\_flags, including NORMAL grades and high-risk grades. The table stores both normal and abnormal evaluated conditions so visit-level clinical status can be reconstructed and reported.Although named risk\_flags, this table stores evaluated risk condition results, not only positive/high-risk flags. |
| Referral workflow data                                           | referrals                                                    | Referral status, type, facility, valid-till, approval state, linked visit/form                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Referral follow-up workflow data                                 | referral\_followups                                          | Follow-up date, facility visited, diagnosis, treatment, outcome, barriers                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Referral/referral FU questions and answers                       | form\_submissions, form\_answers                             | Actual submitted referral/referral follow-up form answers                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Media/evidence                                                   | media\_assets, referral\_followup\_media if added            | Case paper, discharge summary, facility photo, Sakhi-beneficiary proof photo, investigation report                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

**4.0.1 Validation, Rule Audit, and Monitoring Controls**

Include these bullets:

- DB constraints: FK, unique constraints, NOT NULL, enum/check constraints, date order checks, duplicate prevention.
- Application/form validation: mandatory fields, input type, option list, range, conditional skip logic.
- Rule engine validation: risk grade, overall high-risk flag, referral trigger, HR visit trigger, schedule generation.
- Audit trail: every form/rule version change logged with user, timestamp, version, checksum, effective dates.
- Reconciliation checks: compare submitted forms against expected answers/rules and detect missing risk/referral outputs.
- Alerts: rule engine failures, validation failure spikes, sync anomalies, duplicate submissions, missing risk assessment, missing referral for referral-triggering risk.

## Project\_registration\_targets

_Combined definition — this table was previously defined 3 times in this document (this section, and Appendix A at two separate points) with differing column sets. Merged here into one definition carrying the union of every field that appeared in any version; no field from any version was dropped. Where two versions used different names for what appears to be the same concept (e.g. beneficiary\_type\_lookup\_id vs case\_type\_lookup\_id; registration\_target\_count vs registration\_target; status vs status\_lookup\_id), both names are kept as separate rows below pending explicit confirmation of which is authoritative._

##

| Field                            | Data type   | Constraints                                       | Description                                                                             |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| target\_id                       | UUID        | PK NOT NULL                                       | Primary key                                                                             |
| funder\_id FK                    | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> funders.funder\_id                   | Optional if target is funder-level                                                      |
| project\_id FK                   | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> projects.project\_id             | Project for which target is defined                                                     |
| geography\_unit\_id FK           | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> geography\_units.geography\_unit\_id | State/district/block/village/pada scope for the target; NULL means project-level target |
| beneficiary\_type\_lookup\_id FK | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id     | Mother/child/other beneficiary type                                                     |
| case\_type\_lookup\_id FK        | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id     | Mother/Child target split if required; NULL means combined target                       |
| target\_period\_type             | ENUM        | NULL                                              | MONTHLY, QUARTERLY, ANNUAL, PROJECT\_PERIOD                                             |
| target\_period\_start            | DATE        | NOT NULL                                          | Target period start date                                                                |
| target\_period\_end              | DATE        | NOT NULL                                          | Target period end date                                                                  |
| registration\_target\_count      | INT         | NULL CHECK \>= 0                                  | Expected registration target                                                            |
| registration\_target             | INT         | NULL CHECK \>= 0                                  | Planned registration count                                                              |
| status                           | ENUM        | NULL                                              | ACTIVE/INACTIVE                                                                         |
| status\_lookup\_id FK            | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id     | Active, inactive, revised, etc.                                                         |
| created\_at                      | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)            | Audit timestamp                                                                         |
| created\_by\_user\_id            | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                       | Audit user reference                                                                    |
| updated\_at                      | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE  | Audit timestamp                                                                         |
| updated\_by\_user\_id            | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                       | Audit user reference                                                                    |

## beneficiary\_status\_history tracks the lifecycle of a specific program case, not the reusable person/PII record. Therefore it links directly to beneficiary\_cases. The associated PII record can be reached through beneficiary\_cases.pii\_id. A direct FK to beneficiary\_pii is intentionally avoided to prevent ambiguity when one PII record has multiple cases over time

## beneficiary\_cases is the common case header shared across beneficiary types and stores fields common to all cases, such as project, beneficiary type, Sakhi assignment, status, registration date, journey start/end and mother-child linkage. mother\_case\_details and child\_case\_details are type-specific extension tables. This avoids keeping many nullable mother-only and child-only columns in the common case table. In application APIs, these can still be returned as one beneficiary profile object by joining or using beneficiary\_current\_summary, so the UI does not need to perform multiple calls..

| rule\_versions         | Versioned gorules payload and effective dates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | rule\_version\_id    | rule\_set\_id FK, version\_no, rules\_json, effective\_from, effective\_to, published\_by\_user\_id FK, checksum                                                                                                                              | rule\_sets, users                         |
| risk\_conditions       | Master list of clinical risk conditions, grades, stages, referral/education flags.                                                                                                                                                                                                             | risk\_condition\_id  | condition\_code, condition\_name, entity\_type, phase, grade\_scale, referral\_required\_default, education\_required\_default, status                                                                                                        | risk\_flags                               |
| risk\_assessments      | Risk evaluation run after a form submission.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | risk\_assessment\_id | beneficiary\_id FK, visit\_id FK, submission\_id FK, rule\_version\_id FK, evaluated\_at, overall\_risk\_category, hr\_detected\_flag\[visit-level overall high-risk flag derived from the full risk evaluation.\], overall\_high\_risk\_flag | beneficiaries, visits, submissions, rules |
| risk\_flags            | One row per evaluated risk condition at a visit, including both normal and at-risk results. risk\_grade stores NORMAL, MILD, MODERATE, SEVERE, HIGH, CRITICAL, or applicable configured grade. This allows reporting to distinguish evaluated-normal conditions from conditions not evaluated. | risk\_flag\_id       | risk\_assessment\_id FK, risk\_condition\_id FK, risk\_grade, observed\_value\_json, is\_referral\_trigger, is\_education\_trigger, is\_hr\_visit\_trigger                                                                                    | risk\_assessments, risk\_conditions       |
| risk\_state\_snapshots | Longitudinal risk state for baseline/latest/ever-at-risk/CCV transition reporting.                                                                                                                                                                                                             | risk\_state\_id      | beneficiary\_id FK, phase, as\_of\_date, baseline\_risk\_json, latest\_risk\_json, ever\_at\_risk\_json, ccv\_state,source\_visit\_id FK                                                                                                      | beneficiary\_cases, visits                |

## risk\_state\_snapshots is a derived compact snapshot for application state and transition logic. It is not the only source for condition-level reporting. The condition-level source of truth is risk\_flags, where each evaluated risk condition is stored as a separate row linked to the visit and form submission. For efficient reporting, beneficiary\_risk\_condition\_summary should maintain one row per beneficiary per risk condition with baseline, latest, and ever-highest grade/value/date/visit details.

## If a condition is not applicable or not evaluated in a visit, no risk\_flags row is created for that condition unless ARMMAN requires explicit NOT\_EVALUATED tracking.

# **5\. Reporting Layer Definitions**

For analytics and reporting, the same beneficiary-level summary is also exposed through dim\_beneficiary and ClickHouse linelist views. beneficiary\_current\_summary is intended for operational application screens and APIs, while ClickHouse dimensions/views are intended for dashboard and analyst workloads.

| ClickHouse table/view   | Purpose                                                                                                 | Key fields                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| :---------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| dim\_beneficiary        | ClickHouse dimension for beneficiary case, mother/child phase, demographics, project, geography, Sakhi. | beneficiary\_id, case\_type, mother\_id, project\_name, district, block, village, pada, as\_name, supervisor\_name, registration\_date, current\_status                                     |
| dim\_geography          | Denormalized geography hierarchy.                                                                       | geography\_unit\_id, state\_name, district\_name, block\_name, phc\_name, subcentre\_name, village\_name, pada\_name                                                                        |
| dim\_user               | Reporting dimension for Sakhi/Supervisor/Manager.                                                       | user\_id, role, as\_name, supervisor\_name, phone\_masked, project\_name, geography\_scope                                                                                                  |
| fact\_visit             | Visit schedule and completion analytics.                                                                | visit\_id, beneficiary\_id, visit\_type, phase, scheduled\_date, window\_start, window\_end, actual\_visit\_date, status, missed\_flag, hr\_visit\_flag                                     |
| fact\_form\_submission  | Form submission fact with form version and timestamps.                                                  | submission\_id, beneficiary\_id, visit\_id, form\_code, form\_version, submitted\_at, sync\_uploaded\_at, submitted\_by                                                                     |
| fact\_risk\_flag        | Condition-level risk analytics.                                                                         | risk\_flag\_id, beneficiary\_id, visit\_id, risk\_condition, risk\_grade, phase, baseline\_flag, latest\_flag, ever\_at\_risk\_flag, referral\_trigger\_flag                                |
| fact\_referral          | Referral and follow-up analytics.                                                                       | referral\_id, beneficiary\_id, visit\_id, referral\_type, referral\_date, followup\_date, facility\_visited\_flag, lapsed\_flag, accompanied\_approved\_flag, days\_to\_followup            |
| fact\_closure           | Closure and outcome analytics.                                                                          | closure\_id, beneficiary\_id, closure\_type, closure\_reason, event\_date, maternal\_status, child\_status, reopened\_flag                                                                  |
| fact\_incentive         | Wage and payout analytics.                                                                              | incentive\_event\_id, sakhi\_id, event\_month, incentive\_type, source\_entity\_id, quantity, amount\_inr, eligibility\_status                                                              |
| linelist\_\* wide views | Canonical report-ready tables matching dashboard PRD/M\&E field names.                                  | registration\_pw, registration\_child, anc\_visit, pp\_visit, delivery, neonatal\_visit,infant\_child\_visit, mother\_closure, child\_closure, referral, referral\_followup,reopen\_request |

# **6\. Required Indexes and Constraints**

| Area                            | Indexes / constraints                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| :------------------------------ | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Users                           | Unique users.mobile\_number; index user\_roles(user\_id, role\_id, project\_id); index sessions(user\_id, expires\_at).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Geography/project               | Unique geography\_units(parent\_id, geo\_type, code); index project\_geographies(project\_id, geography\_unit\_id); index sakhi\_assignments(sakhi\_id, project\_id, geography\_unit\_id, active\_to).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Beneficiary duplicate detection | Functional/search index over normalized beneficiary name token, DOB, LMP date where applicable, parent/mother identity tokens where applicable, child name token, child DOB, child gender, village/pada, and case\_type. PII remains encrypted; maintain non-reversible search tokens for duplicate checks. For mother enrollment, duplicate detection should consider LMP date in addition to name, DOB/age, geography, and case type to help identify possible duplicate pregnancy registrations while still allowing valid re-enrollment for a later pregnancy. For child enrollment, duplicate detection should consider parent/mother name or linked mother case, child name, child DOB, child gender, and village/pada to identify possible duplicate child registrations while allowing valid sibling records. |
| Care journey                    | Index visit\_schedules(beneficiary\_id, scheduled\_date, status); visit\_instances(sakhi\_id, actual\_visit\_date, status); form\_submissions(beneficiary\_id, form\_definition\_id, submitted\_at).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Risk and referral               | Index risk\_flags(risk\_condition\_id, risk\_grade); risk\_state\_snapshots(beneficiary\_id, as\_of\_date); referrals(beneficiary\_id, status, valid\_till); referral\_followups(referral\_id, followup\_date).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Supervisor monitoring           | Index escalation\_events(assigned\_supervisor\_id, status, created\_at); approval\_requests(approver\_user\_id, status, request\_type); notifications(recipient\_user\_id, status, priority).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Sync                            | Unique sync\_items(local\_entity\_uuid, entity\_type, operation) where possible; index sync\_batches(device\_id, started\_at, status).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Audit                           | Append-only audit\_log with index(entity\_type, entity\_id, created\_at) and actor\_user\_id. Prevent update/delete at application layer.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Reporting                       | ClickHouse partition facts by event month/date; order by project/geography/beneficiary/date to support dashboard filters.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

# **7\. Data Privacy, Retention, and Security**

- Host data within India and encrypt all data at rest and in transit.
- Use application-level encryption/tokenization for beneficiary PII, contact details, RCH number, identity documents, bank details, and media references.
- Record consent at registration and retain consent media link, captured\_by, and timestamp.
- Retain beneficiary health data for at least 7 years post program completion, per SRS. Retain audit logs indefinitely.
- All access to PII, form submissions, approvals, rate changes, rule changes, closure actions, and report exports must create audit\_log entries.
- Right-to-erasure handling should be implemented through legal guidance. Prefer tombstone/anonymization workflows that preserve aggregate reporting integrity.

# **8\. Open Decisions**

| Open item                                           | Database impact                                                                                              |
| :-------------------------------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Final HR thresholds document                        | risk\_conditions, rule\_versions, and risk\_flags may need additional grade/threshold fields.                |
| Critical condition mid-form behavior                | May require persisted interrupted\_form\_attempts if ARMMAN chooses forced exit/discard tracking.            |
| Referral skip button behavior                       | May affect approval\_requests request\_type values, referral statuses, and timeout handling.                 |
| Learn More content package and Marathi translations | content\_sections/topics/versions fields may need richer localization and media-type metadata.               |
| Manager RBAC document                               | May add report\_permissions or role\_report\_scope table beyond generic user\_roles.                         |
| Retainer conditionality and final incentive rates   | May add incentive eligibility rules or additional columns in incentive\_rates/incentive\_events.             |
| Legacy data migration scope                         | May require migration\_staging tables and mapping tables from old IDs to new UUIDs.                          |
| ETL frequency for Supervisor and Manager reports    | Determines Airflow DAG cadence, ClickHouse materialized view refresh policy, and dashboard freshness labels. |

# **9\. Visit Logic and Schedule Generation**

Visit logic is rule-driven and should be implemented through versioned rule\_sets and rule\_versions, not hardcoded in application code. The visit schedule is generated based on beneficiary type, journey phase, anchor dates, previous visit completion/missed status, high-risk state, delivery event, child age, and closure/reopen state.  
The operational schedule is stored in visit\_schedules, while actual visit attempts/completions are stored in visit\_instances. The visit form submission is stored in form\_submissions and linked to the corresponding visit\_id where applicable.  
Visit logic should cover:

- ANC visit generation from LMP/EDD and registration date.
- Postpartum visit generation from delivery date.
- Neonatal visit generation from delivery/child DOB.
- INC visit generation from child age/DOB.
- CCV visit generation from child age and 1000-days continuation rules.
- HR visit creation based on risk rules.
- Missed visit handling and escalation thresholds.
- Delivery event handling, including delivery form, INC/child registration, NN1, and PP1 creation.
- Closure/reopen impact on future scheduled visits.

Each generated visit should record the generated\_by\_rule\_version\_id so that the system can audit which visit logic version produced the schedule.

Visit logic is implemented using rule\_sets, rule\_versions, visit\_schedules, visit\_instances, form\_submissions, risk\_assessments, risk\_flags, risk\_state\_snapshots, referrals, closures, and beneficiary\_status\_history.

### **lookup\_categories**

_Lookup categories master table_

| Column                | Data type    | Constraints | Enum / notes                                                                                                      |
| :-------------------- | :----------- | :---------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| lookup\_category\_id  | UUID         | PK          | NOT NULL                                                                                                          |
| category\_code        | VARCHAR(80)  | NOT NULL    | UNIQUE. Examples: SEX,PHONE\_OWNER, EDUCATION\_LEVEL, VISIT\_STATUS, REFERRAL\_TYPE, RISK\_GRADE, CLOSURE\_REASON |
| category\_name        | VARCHAR(160) | NOT NULL    |                                                                                                                   |
| description           | TEXT         | NULL        |                                                                                                                   |
| is\_active            | BOOLEAN      | NOT NULL    | DEFAULT TRUE                                                                                                      |
| created\_at           | DATETIME(3)  | NOT NULL    | DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)                                                                                     |
| created\_by\_user\_id | UUID         | NULL        | FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                                                            |
| updated\_at           | DATETIME(3)  | NOT NULL    | DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE                                                                           |
| updated\_by\_user\_id | UUID         | NULL        | FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                                                            |

**lookup\_values**

_Lookup values master table_

| Column                    | Data type    | Constraints                         | Enum / notes                                                                     |
| :------------------------ | :----------- | :---------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| lookup\_value\_id         | UUID         | PK                                  | NOT NULL                                                                         |
| lookup\_category\_id      | UUID         | NOT NULL                            | FK \-\> lookup\_categories.lookup\_category\_id                                  |
| value\_code               | VARCHAR(80)  | NOT NULL                            |                                                                                  |
| value\_label              | VARCHAR(160) | NOT NULL                            |                                                                                  |
| sort\_order               | INT          | NOT NULL                            | DEFAULT 0                                                                        |
| parent\_lookup\_value\_id | UUID         | NULL                                | FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id; use when dropdown values are dependent |
| is\_active                | BOOLEAN      | NOT NULL                            | DEFAULT TRUE                                                                     |
| created\_at               | DATETIME(3)  | NOT NULL                            | DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)                                                    |
| created\_by\_user\_id     | UUID         | NULL                                | FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                           |
| updated\_at               | DATETIME(3)  | NOT NULL                            | DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE                                          |
| updated\_by\_user\_id     | UUID         | NULL                                | FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                           |
| UNIQUE                    | \-           | lookup\_category\_id \+ value\_code |                                                                                  |

_All dropdown/select/radio options used in approved forms will be maintained through lookup\_categories and lookup\_values with stable value\_code, display label, and sort\_order. form\_versions.schema\_json stores the published snapshot of these options for offline rendering and version audit, but lookup\_values remains the managed master for option definitions._

### **beneficiary\_cases**

_One program care case for a mother or child._

| Column                        | Data type   | Constraints                                       | Enum / notes                                                                                                                                 |
| :---------------------------- | :---------- | :------------------------------------------------ | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| beneficiary\_id               | UUID        | PK NOT NULL ,                                     | Beneficiary type is stored at the case level through beneficiary\_cases.case\_type\_lookup\_id, not in beneficiary\_pii.                     |
| pii\_id                       | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_pii.pii\_id         |                                                                                                                                              |
| project\_id                   | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> projects.project\_id             |                                                                                                                                              |
| case\_type                    | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                          | MOTHER, CHILD                                                                                                                                |
| pregnancy\_sequence\_no       | SMALLINT    | Null                                              | Used for mother cases to identify first pregnancy, second pregnancy, etc., for the same pii\_id/project\_id where applicable.                |
| previous\_beneficiary\_id     | BIGINT      | Null, FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id  | Links a later pregnancy case to the previous pregnancy case for the same beneficiary person, where known.                                    |
| mother\_beneficiary\_id       | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id   | child linked to mother if enrolled through delivery                                                                                          |
| sakhi\_id                     | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> sakhi\_profiles.sakhi\_id        |                                                                                                                                              |
| registration\_date            | DATE        | NOT NULL                                          |                                                                                                                                              |
| current\_status               | ENUM        | NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'                         | ACTIVE, JOURNEY\_COMPLETE, CLOSED, TRANSFERRED, REOPEN\_REQUESTED                                                                            |
| current\_phase                | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                          | ANC, DELIVERY, PP, NN, INC, CCV, CLOSED                                                                                                      |
| beneficiary\_type\_lookup\_id | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id | Type/category of beneficiary for the program. Example values: PREGNANT\_WOMAN, MOTHER, CHILD, INFANT, depending on final ARMMAN terminology. |
| case\_type\_lookup\_id        | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id | Beneficiary case type for this program, e.g., MOTHER, CHILD. This is the beneficiary type used for journey branching.                        |
| journey\_start\_date          | DATE        | NOT NULL                                          |                                                                                                                                              |
| journey\_end\_date            | DATE        | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                                              |
| created\_at                   | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)            |                                                                                                                                              |
| created\_by\_user\_id         | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                       |                                                                                                                                              |
| updated\_at                   | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE  |                                                                                                                                              |
| updated\_by\_user\_id         | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                       |                                                                                                                                              |
| is\_deleted                   | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                            | soft delete marker                                                                                                                           |
| deleted\_at                   | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                                              |

### **Beneficiary\_risk\_condition\_summary**

_Derived condition-wise risk summary table for each beneficiary case. It supports application screens, MIS, dashboards, and reporting without repeatedly scanning all historical visit-level risk records._

_This is not the source of truth. Source-of-truth remains risk\_assessments and risk\_flags. This table is updated after every applicable visit/risk evaluation._

| Column                                    | Data Type        | Constraint                                            | Description                                                                        |
| :---------------------------------------- | :--------------- | :---------------------------------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| beneficiary\_risk\_condition\_summary\_id | UUID             | PK NOT NULL                                           | Unique summary row ID.                                                             |
| beneficiary\_id                           | UUID             | NOT NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id   | Beneficiary case.                                                                  |
| risk\_condition\_id                       | UUID             | NOT NULL FK \-\> risk\_conditions.risk\_condition\_id | Risk condition, e.g. Anaemia, BP, weight gain.                                     |
| phase                                     | ENUM             | NOT NULL                                              | Program phase: REGISTRATION, ANC, DELIVERY, PP, NN, INFANT\_FOLLOWUP, CLOSURE.     |
| baseline\_grade                           | ENUM/VARCHAR(50) | NULL                                                  | Risk grade at baseline/applicable first assessment for this condition.             |
| baseline\_observed\_value\_json           | JSON             | NULL                                                  | Observed value(s) used for baseline grading, e.g. Hb value.                        |
| baseline\_visit\_id                       | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> visit\_instances.visit\_id               | Visit where baseline value was captured, if visit-based.                           |
| baseline\_submission\_id                  | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> form\_submissions.submission\_id         | Form submission where baseline value came from.                                    |
| baseline\_assessed\_at                    | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                                  | Date/time when baseline risk was assessed.                                         |
| latest\_grade                             | ENUM/VARCHAR(50) | NULL                                                  | Most recent evaluated grade for this condition.                                    |
| latest\_observed\_value\_json             | JSON             | NULL                                                  | Most recent observed value(s).                                                     |
| latest\_visit\_id                         | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> visit\_instances.visit\_id               | Latest visit where condition was evaluated.                                        |
| latest\_submission\_id                    | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> form\_submissions.submission\_id         | Latest form submission where value came from.                                      |
| latest\_assessed\_at                      | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                                  | Date/time of latest assessment.                                                    |
| ever\_highest\_grade                      | ENUM/VARCHAR(50) | NULL                                                  | Highest/worst grade reached for this condition during current beneficiary journey. |
| ever\_highest\_observed\_value\_json      | JSON             | NULL                                                  | Observed value(s) when highest/worst grade was reached.                            |
| ever\_highest\_visit\_id                  | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> visit\_instances.visit\_id               | Visit where highest/worst grade was identified.                                    |
| ever\_highest\_submission\_id             | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> form\_submissions.submission\_id         | Submission where highest/worst grade came from.                                    |
| ever\_highest\_assessed\_at               | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                                  | Date/time when highest/worst grade was identified.                                 |
| ever\_at\_risk\_flag                      | BOOLEAN          | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                | TRUE if this condition ever crossed the configured at-risk/high-risk threshold.    |
| current\_referral\_trigger\_flag          | BOOLEAN          | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                | TRUE if latest condition result currently triggers referral.                       |
| current\_hr\_visit\_trigger\_flag         | BOOLEAN          | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                | TRUE if latest condition result currently triggers high-risk visit scheduling.     |
| source\_rule\_version\_id                 | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> rule\_versions.rule\_version\_id         | Latest rule version used to update this summary.                                   |
| updated\_at                               | DATETIME(3)      | NOT NULL                                              | Last refresh timestamp.                                                            |

### **Beneficiary\_current\_summary**

_beneficiary\_current\_summary should be treated as a derived read model, not the source of truth. Source-of-truth data remains in normalized transactional tables. The summary can be implemented as a database view, materialized table, or application-maintained projection depending on performance needs._

| Column                          | Data type   | Constraints                                       | Enum / notes                                                |
| :------------------------------ | :---------- | :------------------------------------------------ | :---------------------------------------------------------- |
| beneficiary\_id                 | UUID        | PK, FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id    | One row per active/canonical beneficiary case               |
| pii\_id                         | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_pii.pii\_id         | Links encrypted PII record                                  |
| project\_id                     | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> projects.project\_id             |                                                             |
| sakhi\_id                       | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> sakhi\_profiles.sakhi\_id        | Current assigned Sakhi                                      |
| case\_type\_lookup\_id          | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id | Mother/Child                                                |
| current\_status\_lookup\_id     | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id | Active, closed, journey complete, transferred, etc.         |
| dob                             | DATE        | NULL                                              | From PII/child details as applicable                        |
| lmp\_date                       | DATE        | NULL                                              | Mother journey only                                         |
| edd\_date                       | DATE        | NULL                                              | Mother journey only                                         |
| date\_of\_delivery              | DATE        | NULL                                              | Captured from delivery form / derived delivery submission   |
| closure\_date                   | DATE        | NULL                                              | Latest approved closure date                                |
| active\_flag                    | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE                             | Derived from current status                                 |
| overall\_ever\_at\_risk\_flag   | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                            | Derived from risk\_state\_snapshots / historical risk flags |
| latest\_visit\_high\_risk\_flag | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                            | Derived from latest risk\_assessments.hr\_detected\_flag    |
| referral\_active\_flag          | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                            | TRUE when an open/pending referral exists                   |
| last\_visit\_id                 | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> visit\_instances.visit\_id           | Latest completed/attempted visit                            |
| last\_visit\_date               | DATE        | NULL                                              |                                                             |
| state\_id                       | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> states.state\_id                     |                                                             |
| district\_id                    | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> districts.district\_id               |                                                             |
| block\_geography\_unit\_id      | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> geography\_units.geography\_unit\_id | If block is modeled through hierarchy                       |
| panchayat\_id                   | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> panchayats.panchayat\_id             |                                                             |
| village\_geography\_unit\_id    | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> geography\_units.geography\_unit\_id | If village is modeled through hierarchy                     |
| updated\_at                     | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL                                          | Last refresh/update timestamp                               |

### **visit\_schedules**

_Planned visit schedule generated offline and synced._

| Column                           | Data type   | Constraints                                                                                       | Enum / notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :------------------------------- | :---------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| schedule\_id                     | UUID        | PK NOT NULL                                                                                       |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| beneficiary\_id                  | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id                                               |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| visit\_code                      | VARCHAR(40) | NOT NULL                                                                                          | ANC1, ANC-HR, PP1, NN2, INC1, CCV1 etc.Visit label/code such as ANC1, ANC2, ANC-HR1, ANC-HR2, PP1, NN2, INC1, CCV1. High-risk visit codes such as ANC-HR1 and ANC-HR2 represent the sequence of high-risk follow-up visits generated and are not tied to the ANC visit number where the risk was first identified. |
| visit\_type                      | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                                                                          | ANC, ANC\_HR, ANC\_POST\_EDD, DELIVERY, PP, NN, INC, INC\_HR, CCV, CCV\_HR                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| sequence\_no                     | INT         | NULL                                                                                              |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| scheduled\_date                  | DATE        | NOT NULL                                                                                          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| window\_start\_date              | DATE        | NOT NULL                                                                                          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| window\_end\_date                | DATE        | NOT NULL                                                                                          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| anchor\_type                     | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                                                                          | REGISTRATION, LMP, EDD, DELIVERY\_DATE, DOB, ACTUAL\_VISIT, CCV\_TRANSITION                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| anchor\_visit\_id                | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> visit\_instances.visit\_id                                                           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| generated\_by\_rule\_version\_id | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> rule\_versions.rule\_version\_id                                                 |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| status                           | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                                                                          | GENERATED, OPEN, MISSED, COMPLETED, SUPERSEDED, CANCELLED                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| created\_at                      | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)                                                            |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| created\_by\_user\_id            | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                                       |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| trigger\_source\_visit\_id       | BIGINT      | Nullable FK \-\> visits. Visit where the high-risk condition or trigger was identified.           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| trigger\_risk\_assessment\_id    | BIGINT      | Nullable FK \-\> risk\_assessments. Risk assessment that generated the high-risk follow-up visit. |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| updated\_at                      | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE                                                  |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| updated\_by\_user\_id            | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                                                                       |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| is\_deleted                      | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                                                            | soft delete marker                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| deleted\_at                      | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                                                              |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
