risk\_flags

_Condition-level risk flags. Risk evaluation should persist one condition-level result for every configured risk condition evaluated for that visit, even when the result is normal. For example, if HB \= 11 and the Anaemia rule evaluates to Normal, the Anaemia condition result should be stored with risk\_grade \= NORMAL. Referral, HR visit, and education triggers are then derived from the grade and rule flags._

_The risk model is two-level:_

- _risk\_assessments stores the visit/form-level risk evaluation summary, including overall risk category and visit-level high-risk flag._
- _risk\_flags stores one row per evaluated risk condition, such as Anaemia, Hypertension, Diabetes, Low Birth Weight, SAM/MAM, MUW/SUW, danger signs, etc._

_observed\_value\_json stores only the source value(s) relevant to that specific risk condition. For example, Anaemia may store { "hb": 11, "unit": "g/dL" }; Hypertension may store { "systolic": 150, "diastolic": 95 }. The complete visit form payload remains in form\_submissions.form\_data\_json and/or form\_answers._

| Column                 | Data type   | Constraints                                             | Enum / notes                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| :--------------------- | :---------- | :------------------------------------------------------ | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| risk\_flag\_id         | UUID        | PK NOT NULL                                             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| risk\_assessment\_id   | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> risk\_assessments.risk\_assessment\_id |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| risk\_condition\_id    | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> risk\_conditions.risk\_condition\_id   |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| risk\_grade            | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                                | Stores evaluated grade for the condition, including NORMAL, MILD, MODERATE, SEVERE, HIGH, CRITICAL, or equivalent ARMMAN-approved grading values. Normal values must be stored when the condition is evaluated                                                                       |
| observed\_value\_json  | JSON        | NULL                                                    | Stores source measurement/value used for grading, e.g., { "hb": 11, "unit": "g/dL" } for Anaemia. Stores only the measurement/value(s) used for this specific risk condition evaluation, not the entire visit form. Example: Anaemia stores HB value; Hypertension stores BP values. |
| is\_referral\_trigger  | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                  |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| is\_education\_trigger | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                  |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| is\_hr\_visit\_trigger | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                  |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| created\_at            | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)                  |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| created\_by\_user\_id  | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| updated\_at            | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE        |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| updated\_by\_user\_id  | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| is\_deleted            | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                                  | soft delete marker                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| deleted\_at            | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                    |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

| updated\_by\_user\_id   | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                         |                                                                                                                                                                               |

| deleted\_at             | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                |                                                                                                                                                                               |

### **reopen\_requests**

_Reopen request for eligible closed cases._

_When supervisor\_status \= 'REJECTED', decision\_reason\_code\_lookup\_id or decision\_notes must be mandatory. This can be enforced through application validation or a database CHECK constraint depending on implementation capability._

| Column                                          | Data type   | Constraints                                         | Enum / notes                                                                                                |
| :---------------------------------------------- | :---------- | :-------------------------------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| reopen\_request\_id                             | UUID        | PK NOT NULL                                         |                                                                                                             |
| beneficiary\_id                                 | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> beneficiary\_cases.beneficiary\_id |                                                                                                             |
| request\_reason                                 | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                            | MIGRATION\_RETURNED, CLOSED\_BY\_MISTAKE, OTHER                                                             |
| requested\_by\_user\_id                         | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                     |                                                                                                             |
| requested\_at                                   | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL                                            |                                                                                                             |
| supervisor\_status                              | ENUM        | NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'                          | PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED                                                                                 |
| decision\_reason\_code\_lookup\_id              | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id       | Standard coded reason for rejection/approval decision, mainly required when supervisor\_status \= REJECTED. |
| decision\_notes                                 | TEXT        | NULL                                                | Free-text supervisor remarks explaining rejection or approval decision.                                     |
| decided\_by\_user\_id                           | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                         | Supervisor/Admin who made the approval decision.                                                            |
| decided\_at                                     | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                | Timestamp when approval/rejection decision was made.                                                        |
| supervisor\_id                                  | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                         | Supervisor who approved/rejected the reopen request.                                                        |
| supervisor\_rejection\_reason\_code\_lookup\_id | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> lookup\_values.lookup\_value\_id       | Required when supervisor\_status \= REJECTED.                                                               |
| supervisor\_rejection\_notes                    | TEXT        | NULL                                                | Free-text rejection reason. Required when rejected if configured.                                           |
| decided\_at                                     | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                |                                                                                                             |
| created\_at                                     | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)              |                                                                                                             |
| created\_by\_user\_id                           | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                         |                                                                                                             |
| updated\_at                                     | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE    |                                                                                                             |
| updated\_by\_user\_id                           | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                         |                                                                                                             |
| is\_deleted                                     | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                              | soft delete marker                                                                                          |
| deleted\_at                                     | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                                |                                                                                                             |

| approver\_user\_id                     | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                       |                                                                                                                      |
| request\_payload\_json                 | JSON             | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                      |
| status                                 | ENUM             | NOT NULL                                          | PENDING, APPROVED, REJECTED, AUTO\_LAPSED, CANCELLED                                                                 |
| decision\_payload\_json                | JSON             | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                      |
| decided\_at                            | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                      |

| deleted\_at                            | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                              |                                                                                                                      |

| sakhi\_id               | UUID             | NOT NULL FK \-\> sakhi\_profiles.sakhi\_id           |                                                                                                    |
| call\_datetime          | DATETIME(3)      | NOT NULL                                             |                                                                                                    |
| call\_status            | ENUM             | NOT NULL                                             | CONNECTED, NOT\_CONNECTED, FOLLOWUP\_REQUIRED                                                      |
| notes                   | TEXT             | NULL                                                 |                                                                                                    |
| followup\_action        | TEXT             | NULL                                                 |                                                                                                    |
| call\_start\_at         | DATETIME(3)      | NOT NULL                                             | Timestamp when the call was initiated/started.                                                     |
| call\_end\_at           | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                                 | Timestamp when the call ended. NULL if call was not connected or end time was not captured.        |
| call\_duration\_seconds | INT              | NULL CHECK \>= 0                                     | Derived from call\_start\_at and call\_end\_at; can be stored for reporting convenience.           |
| call\_status            | ENUM / lookup FK | NOT NULL                                             | Example values: CONNECTED, NOT\_CONNECTED, FOLLOWUP\_REQUIRED, BUSY, SWITCHED\_OFF, WRONG\_NUMBER. |
| created\_at             | DATETIME(3)      | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)               |                                                                                                    |
| created\_by\_user\_id   | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                          |                                                                                                    |
| updated\_at             | DATETIME(3)      | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE     |                                                                                                    |
| updated\_by\_user\_id   | UUID             | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                          |                                                                                                    |
| is\_deleted             | BOOLEAN          | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                               | soft delete marker                                                                                 |
| deleted\_at             | DATETIME(3)      | NULL                                                 |                                                                                                    |

### **sync\_batches**

_One mobile sync session._

| user\_id              | UUID        | NOT NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                  |                                                |
| direction             | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                         | UPLOAD, DOWNLOAD                               |

| completed\_at         | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                             |                                                |
| status                | ENUM        | NOT NULL                                         | STARTED, COMPLETED, FAILED, PARTIAL, CANCELLED |
| app\_version          | VARCHAR(40) | NULL                                             |                                                |
| network\_type         | ENUM        | NULL                                             | WIFI, MOBILE, OFFLINE, UNKNOWN                 |
| created\_at           | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3)           |                                                |
| created\_by\_user\_id | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                      |                                                |
| updated\_at           | DATETIME(3) | NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT\_TIMESTAMP(3) ON UPDATE |                                                |
| updated\_by\_user\_id | UUID        | NULL FK \-\> users.user\_id                      |                                                |
| is\_deleted           | BOOLEAN     | NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE                           | soft delete marker                             |
| deleted\_at           | DATETIME(3) | NULL                                             |                                                |
